# HackUTD Research: Winning Projects Across 60 Hackathons (Master List v2)

Compiled 2026-11-02 from two research passes (8 agent reports), merged and de-duplicated. This is a LIST of winners with prize/category and links, not an analysis of the projects.

**How to read it:** each row is one prize won; a project that won several prizes appears once per prize. "Category as awarded" is the prize label as Devpost or the organizer words it. "Domain tag" is a short label inferred from the project tagline or name, not an official category, so use it as a rough filter only.

**What changed from v1:** the second pass read the real "Winner <prize>" badges on each project page instead of trusting Devpost's winner filter (which flags 140-370 projects per event, far more than the real 20-50 prize winners). Old rows that showed only "Winner (prize not shown)" were dropped.

## Known Gaps (as reported by the research agents)

- **HackUTD:** Devpost badges show the track a project won (General, T-Mobile, PNC, etc.) but not 1st/2nd/3rd within it, for 2024 and 2025. Confirmed placements exist only for the Pinata prize in 2024 (Monch 1st, Capsule 2nd, Cheddarboxd 3rd). 2024 "Roomie" (General and Hardware) is unverified.
- **Hack the North 2025 and 2026:** all 12 finalists listed each year, but the 1st/2nd/3rd order was not found.
- **HackMIT:** 2026 is complete from HackMIT's own gallery; 2025 has 3 rows and 2024 has 1 row from news articles only.
- **HackGT 13:** "Oracle of the Deep - 1st Place" has no badged project.
- **Junction 2024 and 2025:** one overall winner each (from Wikipedia, unverified); no track winners found.
- **HackEurope:** the first edition was Feb 2026 (no 2025 edition). Only Zerostrike (Grand Winner) and Aegis AI (Stockholm) are listed; Devpost has no badges for the other prizes.
- **HackIllinois 2025:** 1st and 3rd overall not found. **ConUHacks X:** ranks within the Desjardins and Deck prizes not captured.
- **Not yet held or not published:** PennApps XXVII, HackPrinceton Fall 2026, HackHarvard 2026 (no winners yet), BoilerMake XIII, HackUMass XIV (Nov 2026).
- **HackPrix (both seasons), Hack4Bengal 3.0 and 5.0, HackOut 2024:** no winners found. hackCBS, HackOut and Hack4Bengal 2025 list only the overall winners.
- **Sponsor and MLH prizes** are not fully itemized for many editions. Some HackNJIT, DragonHacks and HackNYU editions are older than the organizers' newest event because Devpost has no newer listing.
- **Domain tags** are inferred from names and taglines everywhere, and GitHub links are blank where the project page had none.
